world.elements_static[idx];\n            \n            // Connect to neighbors (up, down, left, right)\n            const int offsets[4][2] = {{1, 0}, {-1, 0}, {0, 1}, {0, -1}};\n            \n            for (int n = 0; n < 4; ++n) {\n                const int nx = x + offsets[n][0];\n                const int ny = y + offsets[n][1];\n                \n                // Check if neighbor is elem.connected_idx[elem.num_connections] = neighbor_idx;\n                    elem.connected_flux[elem.num_connections] = 1.0;\n                    elem.num_connections++;\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n    \n    // Set corner elements as inflow/outflow to create interesting dynamics\n    const int last = n_elems_root - 1;\n    world.elements_static[0 * n_elems_root +    world.elements_static[last * n_elems_root + last].material_idx = INFLOW_MAT_ID;\n}\n\n// Compute energy flux between two elements\ninline val_t computeFlux(const Material& mat, const ElementDynamic& this_elem,\n                        val_t connection_flux, const ElementDynamic& other_elem) {\n    return (other_elem.current_energy - this_elem.current_energy) * \n           mat.transfer_coeff * connection_flux * 0.25;\n}\n\n// Run simulation for n_iters iterations\nvoid runSimulation(World& world, const int n_iters) {\n    const size_t n_elems = elem_static.num_connections; ++j) {\n                const idx_t neighbor_idx = elem_static.connected_idx[j];\n                const ElementDynamic& neighbor_dyn = element state\n            ElementDynamic& elem_write = world.elements_dynamic_swap[i];\n            elem_write.current_energy = elem_dyn.current_energy + total_flux;\n finite\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n    \n    if (std::abs(energy_sum) > energy_epsilon) {\n        printf(\"  WARNING: Energy sum diverged from 0 (expected conservation)\\n\");\n        // buildSquare2D(World& world, const int n_elems_root) {\n    const int n_elems = n_elems_root * n_elems_root;\n    \n    // Initialize materials\n    world.materials.emplace_back(Material{0.8, 0.0}); buildSquare2D(World& world, const int n_elems_root) {\n    const int n_elems = n_elems_root * n_elems_root;\n    \n    // Initialize materials\n    world.materials.emplace_back(Material{0.8, 0.0}); elements) {\n    uint64_t hash = 0;\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < elements.size(); ++i) {\n        // Simple hash combining energy and flux values\n        const uint64_t* e_ptr = reinterpret_cast<const uint64_t*>(&elements[i].current_energy);\n        const uint64_t* f_ptr = reinterpret_cast<const uint64_t*>(&elements[i].total_flux);\n        hash ^= (*e_ptr + i) * 0x9e3779b97f4a7c15ULL;\n elem.connected_flux[elem.num_connections] = 1.0;\n                    elem.num_connections++;\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n    \n    // Set corner elements as inflow/outflow to create interesting dynamics\n    const int last = n_elems_root - 1;\n    world.elements_static[0 * n_elems_root + 0].material_idx = INFLOW_MAT_ID;\n    world.elements_static[0 * n_elems_root + last].material_idx = OUTFLOW_MAT_ID;\n    world.elements_static[last * n_elems_root + 0].material_idx = OUTFLOW_MAT_ID;\n    world.elements_static[last * n_elems_root + last].material_idx = INFLOW_MAT_ID;\n}\n\n// Compute energy flux between two elements\n__device__ inline val_t computeFlux(val_t transfer_coeff, val_t this_energy,\n                                    val_t connection_flux, val_t other_energy) {\n    return (other_energy - this_energy) * transfer_coeff * connection_flux * 0.25;\n}\n\n// Update one element per thread for a single iteration.\n// Connectivity is stored in structure-of-arrays layout (stride n_elems per\n// connection slot) so warp accesses are coalesced.\n__global__ void updateElementsKernel(const uint32_t* __restrict__ num_connections,\n                                     const uint32_t* __restrict__ conn_idx,\n                                     const val_t* __restrict__ conn_flux,\n                                     const val_t* __restrict__ transfer_coeff,\n                                     const val_t* __restrict__ external_flow,\n                                     const val_t* __restrict__ energy_read,\n                                     const val_t* __restrict__ flux_read,\n                                     val_t* __restrict__ energy_write,\n                                     val_t* __restrict__ flux_write,\n                                     const idx_t n_elems) {\n    const idx_t i = static_cast<idx_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;\n    if (i >= n_elems) return;\n\n    const val_t this_energy = energy_read[i];\n    const val_t coeff = transfer_coeff[i];\n\n    // Start with external flow\n    val_t total_flux = external_flow[i];\n\n    // Add flux from all connected elements (same order as the serial code)\n    const uint32_t n_conn = num_connections[i];\n    for (uint32_t j = 0; j < n_conn; ++j) {\n        const idx_t slot = static_cast<idx_t>(j) * n_elems + i;\n        total_flux += computeFlux(coeff, this_energy, conn_flux[slot],\n                                  energy_read[conn_idx[slot]]);\n    }\n\n    // Update element state\n    energy_write[i] = this_energy + total_flux;\n    flux_write[i] = flux_read[i] + fabs(total_flux);\n}\n\n// Run simulation for n_iters iterations on the GPU\nvoid runSimulation(World& world, const int n_iters) {\n    const idx_t n_elems = world.elements_static.size();\n\n    // Repack static data into structure-of-arrays form for coalesced access;\n    // material properties are resolved per element up front.\n    std::vector<uint32_t> h_num_conn(n_elems);\n    std::vector<uint32_t> h_conn_idx(n_elems * MAX_CONNECTIONS);\n    std::vector<val_t> h_conn_flux(n_elems * MAX_CONNECTIONS);\n    std::vector<val_t> h_transfer(n_elems);\n    std::vector<val_t> h_extflow(n_elems);\n    std::vector<val_t> h_energy(n_elems);\n    std::vector<val_t> h_flux(n_elems);\n    for (idx_t i = 0; i < n_elems; ++i) {\n        const ElementStatic& es = world.elements_static[i];\n        const Material& mat = world.materials[es.material_idx];\n        h_num_conn[i] = static_cast<uint32_t>(es.num_connections);\n        for (idx_t j = 0; j < es.num_connections; ++j) {\n            h_conn_idx[j * n_elems + i] = static_cast<uint32_t>(es.connected_idx[j]);\n            h_conn_flux[j * n_elems + i] = es.connected_flux[j];\n        }\n        h_transfer[i] = mat.transfer_coeff;\n        h_extflow[i] = mat.external_flow;\n        h_energy[i] = world.elements_dynamic[i].current_energy;\n        h_flux[i] = world.elements_dynamic[i].total_flux;\n    }\n\n    // Allocate and upload device buffers\n    uint32_t* d_num_conn = nullptr;\n    uint32_t* d_conn_idx = nullptr;\n    val_t* d_conn_flux = nullptr;\n    val_t* d_transfer = nullptr;\n    val_t* d_extflow = nullptr;\n    val_t* d_energy[2] = {nullptr, nullptr};\n    val_t* d_flux[2] = {nullptr, nullptr};\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_num_conn, n_elems * sizeof(uint32_t)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_conn_idx, n_elems * MAX_CONNECTIONS * sizeof(uint32_t)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_conn_flux, n_elems * MAX_CONNECTIONS * sizeof(val_t)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_transfer, n_elems * sizeof(val_t)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_extflow, n_elems * sizeof(val_t)));\n    for (int b = 0; b < 2; ++b) {\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_energy[b], n_elems * sizeof(val_t)));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_flux[b], n_elems * sizeof(val_t)));\n    }\n\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d_num_conn, h_num_conn.data(),\n                          n_elems * sizeof(uint32_t), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d_conn_idx, h_conn_idx.data(),\n                          n_elems * MAX_CONNECTIONS * sizeof(uint32_t), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d_conn_flux, h_conn_flux.data(),\n                          n_elems * MAX_CONNECTIONS * sizeof(val_t), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d_transfer, h_transfer.data(),\n                          n_elems * sizeof(val_t), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d_extflow, h_extflow.data(),\n                          n_elems * sizeof(val_t), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d_energy[0], h_energy.data(),\n                          n_elems * sizeof(val_t), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d_flux[0], h_flux.data(),\n                          n_elems * sizeof(val_t), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n\n    const int block_size = 256;\n    const int grid_size = static_cast<int>((n_elems + block_size - 1) / block_size);\n\n    int read = 0;\n    for (int iter = 0; iter < n_iters; ++iter) {\n        updateElementsKernel<<<grid_size, block_size>>>(\n            d_num_conn, d_conn_idx, d_conn_flux, d_transfer, d_extflow,\n            d_energy[read], d_flux[read], d_energy[1 - read], d_flux[1 - read],\n            n_elems);\n        read = 1 - read;\n    }\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaDeviceSynchronize());\n\n    // Download final state\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(h_energy.data(), d_energy[read],\n                          n_elems * sizeof(val_t), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(h_flux.data(), d_flux[read],\n                          n_elems * sizeof(val_t), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));\n    for (idx_t i = 0; CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_conn_idx));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_conn_flux));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_transfer));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_extflow));\n    for (int b = 0; b < 2; ++b) {\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_energy[b]));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_flux[b]));\n    }\n}\n\n// Validate simulation results\nbool validateResults(const World& world) {\n    val_t energy_sum = 0.0;\n    val_t flux_sum = 0.0;\n    val_t energy_max = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::lowest();\n    val_t energy_min = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::max();\n    \n    for (const auto& elem : world.elements_dynamic) {\n        energy_sum += elem.current_energy;\n        flux_sum += elem.total_flux;\n        energy_max = std::max(elem.current_energy, energy_max);\n        energy_min = std::min(elem.current_energy, energy_min);\n    }\n    \n    printf(\"Validation results:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  Energy sum: %.12f\\n\", energy_sum);\n    printf(\"  Flux sum: %.2f\\n\", flux_sum);\n    printf(\"  Energy range: [%.6f, %.6f]\\n\", energy_min, energy_max);\n    \n    // Check for numerical issues\n    constexpr val_t energy_epsilon = 1e-8;\n    \n    if (!std::isfinite(energy_sum)) {\n        printf(\"  ERROR: Energy sum is not finite\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n    \n    if (std::abs(energy_sum) > energy_epsilon) {\n        printf(\"  WARNING: Energy sum diverged from 0 (expected conservation)\\n\");\n        // Don't fail validation as this can happen with external flows\n    }\n    \n    if (!std::isfinite(flux_sum)) {\n        printf(\"  ERROR: Flux sum is not finite\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n    \n    if (!std::isfinite(energy_max) || !std::isfinite(energy_min)) {\n        printf(\"  ERROR: Energy extrema are not finite\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n    \n    printf(\"  Validation: PASSED\\n\");\n    return true;\n}\n\n// Compute a simple hash of the results for verification\nuint64_t computeHash(const std::vector<ElementDynamic>& elements) {\n    uint64_t hash = 0;\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < elements.size(); ++i) {\n        // Simple hash combining energy and flux values\n        const uint64_t* e_ptr = reinterpret_cast<const uint64_t*>(&elements[i].current_energy);\n        const uint64_t* f_ptr = reinterpret_cast<const uint64_t*>(&elements[i].total_flux);\n        hash ^= (*e_ptr + i) * 0x9e3779b97f4a7c15ULL;\n        hash ^= (*f_ptr + i) * 0xbf58476d1ce4e5b9ULL;\n    }\n    return hash;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Grid size (NxN elements) (default: 512)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -i <num>     Number of simulation iterations (default: 10)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable a 2D square grid as an unstructured mesh\n// This represents computation on arbitrarily-shaped geometries\nvoid buildSquare2D(World& world, const int n_elems_root) {\n    const int n_elems = n_elems_root * n_elems_root;\n    \n    // Initialize materials\n    world.materials.emplace_back(Material{0.8, 0.0});    // Default material\n    world.materials.emplace_back(Material{0.8, 0.5});    // Inflow material\n    world.materials.emplace_back(Material{0.8, -0.5});   // Outflow material\n    \n    // Allocate elements\n    world.elements_static.resize(n_elems);\n    world.elements_dynamic.resize(n_elems);\n    world.elements_dynamic_swap.resize(n_elems);\n    \n    // Initialize all elements with default material and zero energy\n    for (int i = 0; i < n_elems; world.elements_dynamic[i].total_flux = 0.0;\n    }\n    \n    // Build connectivity: each element connects to its neighbors in 2D grid\n    for (int x = 0; x < n_elems_root; ++x) {\n        for (int y = 0; y < n_elems_root; ++y) {\n            const int idx = x * n_elems_root + y;\n            ElementStatic& elem = world.elements_static[idx];\n            \n            // Connect to neighbors (up, down, left, right)\n            const int offsets[4][2] = {{1, 0}, {-1, 0}, {0, 1}, {0, -1}};\n            \n            for (int n = 0; n < 4; ++n) {\n                const int nx = x + elem.connected_flux[elem.num_connections] = 1.0;\n                    elem.num_connections++;\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n    \n    // Set corner elements as inflow/outflow to create interesting dynamics\n    const int last = n_elems_root - 1;\n    world.elements_static[0 * n_elems_root + 0].material_idx = INFLOW_MAT_ID;\n    world.elements_static[0 * n_elems_root + connection_flux, val_t other_energy) {\n    return (other_energy - this_energy) * transfer_coeff * connection_flux * 0.25;\n}\n\n// Update one element per thread for a single iteration.\n// return;\n\n    const val_t this_energy = energy_read[i];\n    const val_t coeff = transfer_coeff[i];\n\n    // Start with external flow\n    val_t total_flux = external_flow[i];\n\n    // Add flux from all connected elements (same order as the serial code)\n    const uint32_t n_conn = num_connections[i];\n    for (uint32_t j = 0; j < n_conn; ++j) {\n        const idx_t slot = element state\n    energy_write[i] = this_energy + total_flux;\n    flux_write[i] = flux_read[i] + fabs(total_flux);\n}\n\n// Run simulation for n_iters iterations on the GPU\nvoid runSimulation(World& world, const int n_iters) {\n    const idx_t n_elems = world.elements_static.size();\n\n    // Repack static data into structure-of-arrays form for coalesced access;\n    // material properties are resolved per element up front. Only the slots\n    // actually used by the mesh (max connection count) are stored. The repack\n    // is embarrassingly parallel, so spread it over host threads.\n    const size_t n_threads = std::max(1u, std::thread::hardware_concurrency());\n    auto parallelFor = [&](auto&& body) {\n        std::vector<std::thread> threads;\n        threads.reserve(n_threads);\n        for (size_t t = 0; t < n_threads; ++t) {\n            const idx_t begin = n_elems * t / n_threads;\n CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_energy[b], n_elems * sizeof(val_t)));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_flux[b], n_elems * sizeof(val_t)));\n    }\n\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d_num_conn, = 256;\n    const int grid_size = static_cast<int>((n_elems + block_size - 1) / block_size);\n\n    int read = 0;\n    for (int iter = 0; iter < n_iters; ++iter) {\n        updateElementsKernel<<<grid_size, block_size>>>(\n            d_num_conn, d_conn_idx, d_conn_flux, d_transfer, d_extflow,\n            d_energy[read], d_flux[read], d_energy[1 - read], d_flux[1 - CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(h_energy.data(), d_energy[read],\n                          n_elems * sizeof(val_t), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(h_flux.data(), d_flux[read],\n world.elements_dynamic[i].total_flux = h_flux[i];\n    }\n\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_num_conn));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_conn_idx));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_conn_flux));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_transfer));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_extflow));\n    for (int b = 0; b < 2; ++b) {\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_energy[b]));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_flux[b]));\n    }\n}\n\n// Validate simulation results\nbool validateResults(const World& world) {\n    val_t energy_sum = 0.0;\n    val_t flux_sum = 0.0;\n    val_t energy_max = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::lowest();\n    val_t energy_min = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::max();\n    \n    for (const auto& elem : world.elements_dynamic) {\n        energy_sum += elem.current_energy;\n        flux_sum += elem.total_flux;\n        energy_max = std::max(elem.current_energy, energy_max);\n        energy_min = std::min(elem.current_energy, energy_min);\n    }\n    \n    printf(\"Validation results:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  Energy sum: %.12f\\n\", energy_sum);\n    printf(\"  Flux sum: %.2f\\n\", flux_sum);\n    printf(\"  Energy range: [%.6f, %.6f]\\n\", finite\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n    \n    if (std::abs(energy_sum) > energy_epsilon) {\n        printf(\"  WARNING: Energy sum diverged from 0 (expected conservation)\\n\");\n        // Don't fail validation as this can happen with external flows\n    }\n    \n    if (!std::isfinite(flux_sum)) {\n        printf(\"  ERROR: Flux sum is not finite\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n    \n    if (!std::isfinite(energy_max) || !std::isfinite(energy_min)) {\n        printf(\"  ERROR: Energy extrema are not finite\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n    \n    printf(\"  Validation: PASSED\\n\");\n    return true;\n}\n\n// Compute a simple hash of the results for verification\nuint64_t computeHash(const std::vector<ElementDynamic>& elements) {\n    uint64_t hash = 0;\n    for material properties are resolved per element up front. Only the slots\n    // actually used by the mesh (max connection count) are stored. }\n}\n\n// Validate simulation results\nbool validateResults(const World& world) {\n    val_t energy_sum = 0.0;\n    val_t flux_sum = 0.0;\n    val_t energy_max = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::lowest();\n    val_t energy_min = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::max();\n    \n    for (const auto& elem : world.elements_dynamic) {\n        energy_sum += \n    printf(\"Validation results:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  Energy sum: %.12f\\n\", energy_sum);\n    printf(\"  Flux sum: %.2f\\n\", flux_sum);\n    printf(\"  Energy range: [%.6f, %.6f]\\n\",   \n    if (!std::isfinite(energy_max) || !std::isfinite(energy_min)) {\n        printf(\"  ERROR: Energy extrema are not finite\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n    \n    printf(\"  Validation: PASSED\\n\");\n    return true;\n}\n\n// Compute a simple hash of the results for verification\nuint64_t computeHash(const std::vector<ElementDynamic>& elements) {\n    uint64_t hash = 0;\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < elements.size(); ++i) {\n        // Simple hash combining energy and flux values\n        const uint64_t* e_ptr = reinterpret_cast<const uint64_t*>(&elements[i].current_energy);\n        const uint64_t* f_ptr = reinterpret_cast<const uint64_t*>(&elements[i].total_flux);\n        hash ^= (*e_ptr + i) * 0x9e3779b97f4a7c15ULL;\n        hash ^= (*f_ptr + i) * 0xbf58476d1ce4e5b9ULL;\n material properties are resolved per element up front. Only the slots\n    // actually used by the mesh (max connection count) are stored. The repack\n    // is embarrassingly parallel, so spread it over host threads.\n    const size_t n_threads = std::max(1u, std::thread::hardware_concurrency());\n    auto parallelFor = [&](auto&& body) }\n}\n\n// Validate simulation results\nbool validateResults(const World& world) {\n    val_t energy_sum = 0.0;\n    val_t flux_sum = 0.0;\n    val_t energy_max = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::lowest();\n    val_t energy_min = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::max();\n    \n    for (const auto& elem : world.elements_dynamic) {\n        energy_sum += \n    printf(\"Validation results:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  Energy sum: %.12f\\n\", energy_sum);\n    printf(\"  Flux sum: %.2f\\n\", flux_sum);\n    printf(\"  Energy range: [%.6f, %.6f]\\n\",   \n    if (!std::isfinite(energy_max) || !std::isfinite(energy_min)) {\n        printf(\"  ERROR: Energy extrema are not finite\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n    \n    printf(\"  Validation: // External energy source/sink\n};\n\n// Static connectivity information for each element\nstruct ElementStatic {\n    idx_t material_idx;\n    idx_t num_connections;\n    idx_t buildSquare2D(World& world, const int n_elems_root) {\n    const int n_elems = n_elems_root * n_elems_root;\n    \n    // Initialize materials\n    world.materials.emplace_back(Material{0.8, 0.0});    // Default material\n    world.materials.emplace_back(Material{0.8, 0.5});    // Inflow material\n    world.materials.emplace_back(Material{0.8, -0.5});   // Outflow material\n    \n    // Allocate elements\n    world.elements_static.resize(n_elems);\n    world.elements_dynamic.resize(n_elems);\n    world.elements_dynamic_swap.resize(n_elems);\n    \n    // Initialize all elements with default material and zero energy\n    for (int i = 0; i < n_elems; ++i) {\n        world.elements_static[i].material_idx = DEFAULT_MAT_ID;\n        world.elements_static[i].num_connections = 0;\n        world.elements_dynamic[i].current_energy = 0.0;\n        world.elements_dynamic[i].total_flux = 0.0;\n    }\n    \n    // Build connectivity: each element connects to its neighbors in 2D grid\n    for (int x = 0; x < n_elems_root; ++x) {\n        for (int y = 0; y < n_elems_root; ++y) {\n            const int idx = x * n_elems_root + y;\n            ElementStatic& elem = world.elements_static[idx];\n            \n            // Connect to neighbors (up, down, left, right)\n            const int offsets[4][2] = {{1, 0}, {-1, 0}, {0, 1}, {0, -1}};\n            \n            for (int n = 0; n < 4; ++n) {\n                const int nx = x + offsets[n][0];\n                const int ny = y + offsets[n][1];\n                \n                // Check if neighbor is within bounds\n                if (nx >= 0 && nx < n_elems_root && ny >= 0 && ny < n_elems_root) {\n                    const int neighbor_idx = nx * n_elems_root + ny;\n                    elem.connected_idx[elem.num_connections] = neighbor_idx;\n                    elem.connected_flux[elem.num_connections] = 1.0;\n                    elem.num_connections++;\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n    \n    // Set corner elements as inflow/outflow to create interesting dynamics\n    const int last = n_elems_root - 1;\n    world.elements_static[0 * n_elems_root + 0].material_idx = INFLOW_MAT_ID;\n    world.elements_static[0 * n_elems_root + last].material_idx = OUTFLOW_MAT_ID;\n    world.elements_static[last * n_elems_root + 0].material_idx = OUTFLOW_MAT_ID;\n    world.elements_static[last * n_elems_root + last].material_idx = INFLOW_MAT_ID;\n}\n\n// Compute energy flux between two elements\n__device__ inline val_t computeFlux(val_t transfer_coeff, val_t this_energy,\n                                    val_t connection_flux, val_t other_energy) {\n    return (other_energy - this_energy) * transfer_coeff * connection_flux * 0.25;\n}\n\n// Update one element per thread for a single iteration.\n// Connectivity is stored in structure-of-arrays layout (stride n_elems per\n// connection slot) so warp accesses are coalesced.\n__global__ void updateElementsKernel(const uint32_t* __restrict__ num_connections,\n                                     const uint32_t* __restrict__ conn_idx,\n                                     const val_t* __restrict__ conn_flux,\n                                     const val_t* __restrict__ transfer_coeff,\n                                     const val_t* __restrict__ external_flow,\n                                     const val_t* __restrict__ energy_read,\n                                     const val_t* __restrict__ flux_read,\n                                     val_t* __restrict__ energy_write,\n                                     val_t* __restrict__ flux_write,\n                                     const idx_t n_elems) {\n    const idx_t i = static_cast<idx_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;\n    if (i >= n_elems) return;\n\n    const val_t this_energy = energy_read[i];\n    const val_t coeff = transfer_coeff[i];\n\n    // Start with external flow\n    val_t total_flux = external_flow[i];\n\n    // Add flux from all connected elements (same order as the serial code)\n    const uint32_t n_conn = num_connections[i];\n    for (uint32_t j = 0; j < n_conn; ++j) {\n        const idx_t slot = static_cast<idx_t>(j) * n_elems + i;\n        total_flux += computeFlux(coeff, this_energy, conn_flux[slot],\n                                  energy_read[conn_idx[slot]]);\n    }\n\n    // Update element state\n    energy_write[i] = this_energy + total_flux;\n    flux_write[i] = flux_read[i] + fabs(total_flux);\n}\n\n// Run simulation for n_iters iterations on the GPU\nvoid runSimulation(World& world, const int n_iters) {\n    const idx_t n_elems = world.elements_static.size();\n\n    // Repack static data into structure-of-arrays form for coalesced access;\n    // material properties are resolved per element up front. Only the slots\n    // actually used by the mesh (max connection count) are stored. The repack\n    // is embarrassingly parallel, so spread it over host threads.\n    const size_t n_threads = std::max(1u, std::thread::hardware_concurrency());\n    auto parallelFor = [&](auto&& body) {\n        std::vector<std::thread> threads;\n        threads.reserve(n_threads);\n        for (size_t t = 0; t < n_threads; ++t) {\n            const idx_t begin = n_elems * t / n_threads;\n            const idx_t end = n_elems * (t + 1) / n_threads;\n            threads.emplace_back(body, t, begin, end);\n        }\n        for (auto& th : threads) th.join();\n    };\n\n    std::vector<idx_t> val_t* __restrict__ flux_write,\n                                     const idx_t n_elems) {\n    const idx_t i = static_cast<idx_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;\n    if (i >= n_elems) return;\n\n    const val_t this_energy = energy_read[i];\n    const val_t coeff = transfer_coeff[i];\n\n    // Start with external flow\n    val_t total_flux = external_flow[i];\n\n    // Add flux from all connected elements (same order as the serial code)\n    const uint32_t n_conn = num_connections[i];\n    for (uint32_t j = 0; j < n_conn; ++j) {\n        const idx_t slot = static_cast<idx_t>(j) * n_elems + i;\n        total_flux += computeFlux(coeff, this_energy, conn_flux[slot],\n                                  energy_read[conn_idx[slot]]);\n    }\n\n    // Update element state\n    energy_write[i] = this_energy + total_flux;\n    flux_write[i] = flux_read[i] + fabs(total_flux);\n}\n\n// Run simulation for n_iters iterations on the GPU\nvoid runSimulation(World& world, const int n_iters) {\n    const idx_t n_elems = world.elements_static.size();\n\n    // Repack static data into structure-of-arrays form for coalesced access;\n    // material properties are resolved per element up front. Only the slots\n    // actually used by the mesh (max connection count) are stored. The repack\n    // is embarrassingly parallel, so spread it over host threads.\n    const size_t n_threads = std::max(1u, std::thread::hardware_concurrency());\n    auto parallelFor = [&](auto&& body) {\n        std::vector<std::thread> threads;\n        threads.reserve(n_threads);\n        for (size_t t = 0; t < n_threads; ++t) {\n            const idx_t begin = n_elems * t / n_threads;\n            const idx_t end = n_elems * (t + 1) / n_threads;\n            threads.emplace_back(body, t, begin, end);\n        }\n        for (auto& th : threads) th.join();\n    };\n\n    std::vector<idx_t> thread_max_conn(n_threads, 0);\n    parallelFor([&](size_t t, idx_t begin, idx_t end) {\n        idx_t local_max = 0;\n        for (idx_t i = begin; i < end; ++i) {\n            local_max = std::max(local_max, world.elements_static[i].num_connections);\n        }\n        thread_max_conn[t] = local_max;\n    });\n    idx_t max_conn = 0;\n    for (idx_t m : thread_max_conn) max_conn = std::max(max_conn, m);\n\n    std::vector<uint32_t> h_num_conn(n_elems);\n    std::vector<uint32_t> h_conn_idx(n_elems * max_conn);\n    std::vector<val_t> h_conn_flux(n_elems * max_conn);\n    std::vector<val_t> h_transfer(n_elems);\n    std::vector<val_t> h_extflow(n_elems);\n    std::vector<val_t> h_energy(n_elems);\n    std::vector<val_t> h_flux(n_elems);\n    parallelFor([&](size_t, idx_t begin, idx_t end) {\n        for (idx_t i = begin; i < end; ++i) {\n            const ElementStatic& es = world.elements_static[i];\n            const Material& mat = world.materials[es.material_idx];\n            h_num_conn[i] = static_cast<uint32_t>(es.num_connections);\n            for (idx_t j = 0; j < es.num_connections; ++j) {\n                h_conn_idx[j * n_elems + i] = static_cast<uint32_t>(es.connected_idx[j]);\n                h_conn_flux[j * n_elems + i] = es.connected_flux[j];\n            }\n            h_transfer[i] = mat.transfer_coeff;\n            h_extflow[i] = mat.external_flow;\n            h_energy[i] = world.elements_dynamic[i].current_energy;\n            h_flux[i] = world.elements_dynamic[i].total_flux;\n        }\n    });\n\n    // Allocate and upload device buffers\n    uint32_t* d_num_conn = nullptr;\n    uint32_t* d_conn_idx = nullptr;\n    val_t* d_conn_flux = nullptr;\n    val_t* d_transfer = nullptr;\n    val_t* d_extflow = nullptr;\n    val_t* d_energy[2] = {nullptr, nullptr};\n    val_t* d_flux[2] = {nullptr, nullptr};\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_num_conn, n_elems * sizeof(uint32_t)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_conn_idx, n_elems * max_conn * sizeof(uint32_t)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_conn_flux, n_elems * max_conn * sizeof(val_t)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_transfer, n_elems * sizeof(val_t)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_extflow, n_elems * sizeof(val_t)));\n    for (int b = 0; b < 2; ++b) {\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_energy[b], n_elems * sizeof(val_t)));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_flux[b], n_elems * sizeof(val_t)));\n    }\n\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d_num_conn, h_num_conn.data(),\n                          n_elems * sizeof(uint32_t), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d_conn_idx, h_conn_idx.data(),\n                          n_elems * max_conn * sizeof(uint32_t), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d_conn_flux, h_conn_flux.data(),\n                          n_elems * max_conn * sizeof(val_t), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d_transfer, h_transfer.data(),\n                          n_elems * sizeof(val_t), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d_extflow, h_extflow.data(),\n                          n_elems * sizeof(val_t), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d_energy[0], h_energy.data(),\n                          n_elems * sizeof(val_t), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n static_cast<int>((n_elems + block_size - 1) / block_size);\n\n    int read = 0;\n    for (int iter = 0; iter < n_iters; ++iter) {\n        updateElementsKernel<<<grid_size, block_size>>>(\n            d_num_conn, d_conn_idx, d_conn_flux, d_transfer, d_extflow,\n            d_energy[read], d_flux[read], d_energy[1 - read], d_flux[1 - read],\n            n_elems);\n        read = 1 - read;\n    }\n CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_energy[b]));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_flux[b]));\n    }\n}\n\n// Validate simulation results\nbool validateResults(const World& world) {\n    val_t energy_sum = 0.0;\n    val_t flux_sum = 0.0;\n    val_t energy_max = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::lowest();\n    val_t energy_min = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::max();\n    \n    for (const auto& elem : world.elements_dynamic) {\n        energy_sum += elem.current_energy;\n        flux_sum += elem.total_flux;\n        energy_max = std::max(elem.current_energy, energy_max);\n        energy_min = false;\n    }\n    \n    printf(\"  Validation: PASSED\\n\");\n    return true;\n}\n\n// Compute a simple hash of the results for verification\nuint64_t computeHash(const std::vector<ElementDynamic>& elements) {\n    uint64_t hash = 0;\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < elements.size(); ++i) {\n        // Simple hash combining energy and flux values\n        const uint64_t* e_ptr = reinterpret_cast<const uint64_t*>(&elements[i].current_energy);\n        const uint64_t* f_ptr = reinterpret_cast<const uint64_t*>(&elements[i].total_flux);\n        hash ^= (*e_ptr + i) * 0x9e3779b97f4a7c15ULL;\n per element (for a 2D grid: 4 neighbors)\nconstexpr int MAX_CONNECTIONS = 8;\n\n// Material properties for energy transfer\nstruct Material {\n    val_t transfer_coeff;  // Energy transfer coefficient\n    val_t external_flow;   // External energy source/sink\n};\n\n// Static connectivity information for each element\nstruct ElementStatic {\n    idx_t material_idx;\n    idx_t std::vector<ElementStatic> elements_static;\n    std::vector<ElementDynamic> elements_dynamic;\n    std::vector<ElementDynamic> elements_dynamic_swap;\n};\n\n// Material type IDs\nconstexpr idx_t DEFAULT_MAT_ID = 0;\nconstexpr idx_t INFLOW_MAT_ID = 1;\nconstexpr idx_t OUTFLOW_MAT_ID = 2;\n\n// Build a 2D square grid as an unstructured mesh\n// This represents computation on arbitrarily-shaped geometries\nvoid buildSquare2D(World& world, const int n_elems_root) {\n    const int n_elems = n_elems_root * n_elems_root;\n    \n    // Initialize materials\n    world.materials.emplace_back(Material{0.8, 0.0});    // Default material\n    world.materials.emplace_back(Material{0.8, 0.5});    // Inflow material\n    world.materials.emplace_back(Material{0.8, -0.5});   // Outflow material\n    \n    // Allocate elements\n    world.elements_static.resize(n_elems);\n    world.elements_dynamic.resize(n_elems);\n    world.elements_dynamic_swap.resize(n_elems);\n    \n    // Initialize all elements with default material and zero energy\n    for (int i = 0; i < n_elems; ++i) {\n        world.elements_static[i].material_idx = DEFAULT_MAT_ID;\n        world.elements_static[i].num_connections = 0;\n        world.elements_dynamic[i].current_energy = 0.0;\n        world.elements_dynamic[i].total_flux = 0.0;\n    }\n    \n    // Build connectivity: each element connects to its neighbors in 2D grid\n    for (int x = 0; x < n_elems_root; ++x) {\n        for (int y = 0; y < n_elems_root; ++y) {\n            const int idx = x * n_elems_root + y;\n            ElementStatic& elem = world.elements_static[idx];\n            \n            // Connect to neighbors (up, down, left, right)\n            const int offsets[4][2] = {{1, 0}, {-1, 0}, {0, 1}, {0, -1}};\n            \n            for (int n = 0; n < 4; ++n) {\n                const int nx = x + offsets[n][0];\n                const int ny = y + offsets[n][1];\n                \n                // Check if neighbor is within bounds\n                if (nx >= 0 && nx < n_elems_root && ny >= 0 elem.connected_flux[elem.num_connections] = 1.0;\n                    elem.num_connections++;\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n    \n    // Set corner elements as inflow/outflow to create interesting dynamics\n    const int last = n_elems_root - 1;\n    world.elements_static[0 * n_elems_root + 0].material_idx = INFLOW_MAT_ID;\n    world.elements_static[0 * n_elems_root + connection_flux, val_t other_energy) {\n    return (other_energy - this_energy) * transfer_coeff * connection_flux * 0.25;\n}\n\n// Update one element per thread for a single iteration.\n// Connectivity is stored in structure-of-arrays layout (stride n_elems per\n// connection slot) so warp accesses are coalesced.\n__global__ void updateElementsKernel(const uint32_t* __restrict__ num_connections,\n                                     const uint32_t* __restrict__ conn_idx,\n                                     const val_t* __restrict__ conn_flux,\n                                     const val_t* __restrict__ transfer_coeff,\n                                     const val_t* __restrict__ external_flow,\n                                     const val_t* __restrict__ from all connected elements (same order as the serial code)\n    const uint32_t n_conn = num_connections[i];\n    for (uint32_t j = 0; j < n_conn; ++j) {\n        const idx_t slot = static_cast<idx_t>(j) * n_elems + i;\n        total_flux += computeFlux(coeff, this_energy, conn_flux[slot],\n                                  energy_read[conn_idx[slot]]);\n    }\n\n    // Update element state\n    energy_write[i] = this_energy + total_flux;\n    flux_write[i] = flux_read[i] + fabs(total_flux);\n}\n\n// Run simulation for n_iters iterations on the GPU\nvoid runSimulation(World& world, const int n_iters) {\n    auto tprev = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    auto lap = [&](const char* name){ auto now=std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now(); fprintf(stderr, \"  [%s] %.2f ms\\n\", name, std::chrono::duration<double,std::milli>(now-tprev).count()); tprev=now; };\n    const idx_t n_elems = world.elements_static.size();\n\n    // Repack static data into structure-of-arrays form for coalesced access;\n    // material properties are resolved per element up front. Only the slots\n    // actually used by the mesh (max connection count) are stored. The repack\n    // is embarrassingly parallel, so spread it over host threads.\n    // Thread spawn overhead dominates below ~1M elements, and ~16 threads\n    // saturate host memory bandwidth for this streaming repack.\n    const size_t n_threads = std::max<size_t>(\n        1, std::min<size_t>({16, std::thread::hardware_concurrency(),\n                             n_elems / (256 * 1024)}));\n begin = n_elems * t / n_threads;\n            const idx_t end = n_elems * (t + 1) / n_threads;\n            threads.emplace_back(body, t, begin, end);\n        }\n        for (auto& th : threads) (idx_t m : thread_max_conn) max_conn = std::max(max_conn, m);\n    lap(\"maxconn\");\n\n    std::vector<uint32_t> h_num_conn(n_elems);\n    std::vector<uint32_t> h_conn_idx(n_elems * max_conn);\n std::vector<val_t> h_flux(n_elems);\n    lap(\"hostalloc\");\n    parallelFor([&](size_t, idx_t begin, idx_t end) {\n        for (idx_t i = begin; i < end; ++i) {\n            const ElementStatic& es CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_num_conn, n_elems * sizeof(uint32_t)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_conn_idx, n_elems * max_conn * sizeof(uint32_t)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_conn_flux, n_elems * max_conn * sizeof(val_t)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_transfer, n_elems * sizeof(val_t)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_extflow, n_elems * sizeof(val_t)));\n    for (int b = 0; b < 2; ++b) {\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_energy[b], n_elems * sizeof(val_t)));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_flux[b], n_elems * sizeof(val_t)));\n    }\n\n    lap(\"cudamalloc\");\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d_num_conn, h_num_conn.data(),\n                          n_elems * sizeof(uint32_t), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d_conn_idx, h_conn_idx.data(),\n                          n_elems * max_conn * sizeof(uint32_t), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d_conn_flux, h_conn_flux.data(),\n                          n_elems * max_conn * sizeof(val_t), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d_transfer, h_transfer.data(),\n                          n_elems * sizeof(val_t), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d_extflow, h_extflow.data(),\n                          n_elems * sizeof(val_t), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d_energy[0], h_energy.data(),\n                          n_elems * sizeof(val_t), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d_flux[0], h_flux.data(),\n                          n_elems * sizeof(val_t), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n\n    lap(\"h2d\");\n    const int block_size = 256;\n    const int grid_size = static_cast<int>((n_elems + block_size - 1) / block_size);\n\n    int read = 0;\n    for (int iter = 0; iter < n_iters; ++iter) {\n        updateElementsKernel<<<grid_size, block_size>>>(\n            d_num_conn, d_conn_idx, d_conn_flux, d_transfer, d_extflow,\n            d_energy[read], d_flux[read], d_energy[1 - Download final state\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(h_energy.data(), d_energy[read],\n                          n_elems * sizeof(val_t), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(h_flux.data(), h_energy[i];\n        world.elements_dynamic[i].total_flux = h_flux[i];\n    }\n\n    lap(\"d2h\");\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_num_conn));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_conn_idx));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_conn_flux));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_transfer));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_extflow));\n    for (int b = 0; b < 2; ++b) {\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_energy[b]));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_flux[b]));\n    }\n}\n\n// Validate simulation results\nbool validateResults(const World& world) {\n    val_t energy_sum = 0.0;\n    val_t flux_sum = 0.0;\n    val_t energy_max = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::lowest();\n    val_t energy_min = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::max();\n    \n    for (const auto& elem : world.elements_dynamic) {\n        energy_sum += on arbitrarily-shaped geometries\nvoid buildSquare2D(World& world, const int n_elems_root) {\n    const int n_elems = n_elems_root * n_elems_root;\n    \n    // Initialize materials\n    world.materials.emplace_back(Material{0.8, 0.0});    // Default material\n    world.materials.emplace_back(Material{0.8, 0.5});    // Inflow material\n    world.materials.emplace_back(Material{0.8, world.elements_dynamic[i].total_flux = 0.0;\n    }\n    \n    // Build connectivity: each element connects to its neighbors in 2D grid\n    for (int x = 0; x < n_elems_root; ++x) {\n        for (int y down, left, right)\n            const int offsets[4][2] = {{1, 0}, {-1, 0}, {0, 1}, {0, -1}};\n            \n            for (int n = 0; n < 4; ++n) {\n                const int nx = x + num_connections,\n                                     const uint32_t* __restrict__ conn_idx,\n                                     const val_t* __restrict__ conn_flux,\n from all connected elements (same order as the serial code)\n    const uint32_t n_conn = num_connections[i];\n    for (uint32_t j = 0; j < n_conn; ++j) {\n        const idx_t slot = static_cast<idx_t>(j) * n_elems + i;\n        total_flux += computeFlux(coeff, this_energy, conn_flux[slot],\n                                  energy_read[conn_idx[slot]]);\n    }\n\n    // Update element state\n    energy_write[i] = this_energy + total_flux;\n    flux_write[i] = flux_read[i] + fabs(total_flux);\n}\n\n// Run simulation for n_iters iterations on the GPU\nvoid runSimulation(World& world, const int n_iters) {\n    auto tprev = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    auto lap = [&](const char* name){ auto now=std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now(); fprintf(stderr, \"  [%s] %.2f ms\\n\", name, std::chrono::duration<double,std::milli>(now-tprev).count()); tprev=now; };\n    const idx_t n_elems = world.elements_static.size();\n\n    // Repack static data into structure-of-arrays form for coalesced access;\n    // material properties are resolved per element up front. Only the slots\n    // actually used by the mesh (max connection count) are stored. The repack\n    // is embarrassingly parallel, so spread it over host threads.\n    // Thread spawn overhead dominates below ~1M elements, and ~16 threads\n    // saturate host memory bandwidth for this streaming repack.\n    const size_t n_threads = std::max<size_t>(\n        1, std::min<size_t>({16, std::thread::hardware_concurrency(),\n                             n_elems / (256 * 1024)}));\n begin = n_elems * t / n_threads;\n            const idx_t end = n_elems * (t + 1) / n_threads;\n            threads.emplace_back(body, t, begin, end);\n        }\n        for (auto& th : threads) // Inflow material\n    world.materials.emplace_back(Material{0.8, -0.5});   // Outflow material\n    \n    // Allocate elements\n    world.elements_static.resize(n_elems);\n    world.elements_dynamic.resize(n_elems);\n    world.elements_dynamic_swap.resize(n_elems);\n    \n    // Initialize all elements with default material and zero energy\n    for (int i = 0; i < n_elems; elem.connected_flux[elem.num_connections] = 1.0;\n                    elem.num_connections++;\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n    \n    // Set corner elements as inflow/outflow to connection_flux, val_t other_energy) {\n    return (other_energy - this_energy) * transfer_coeff * connection_flux * 0.25;\n}\n\n// Update one element per thread for a single iteration.\n// Connectivity is stored in structure-of-arrays layout (stride n_elems per\n// connection slot) so warp accesses are coalesced.\n__global__ void updateElementsKernel(const uint32_t* __restrict__ num_connections,\n                                     const uint32_t* __restrict__ conn_idx,\n                                     const val_t* __restrict__ conn_flux,\n                                     const val_t* __restrict__ transfer_coeff,\n                                     const val_t* __restrict__ external_flow,\n                                     const val_t* __restrict__ runSimulation(World& world, const int n_iters) {\n    auto tprev = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    auto lap = [&](const char* name){ auto now=std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now(); Only the slots\n    // actually used by the mesh (max connection count) are stored. The repack\n    // is embarrassingly parallel, so spread it over host threads.\n    // Thread spawn overhead dominates below ~1M elements, and ~16 threads\n    // saturate host memory bandwidth for this streaming repack.\n    const size_t n_threads = std::max<size_t>(\n        1, std::min<size_t>({16, std::thread::hardware_concurrency(),\n                             n_elems / (256 * 1024)}));\n std::unique_ptr<uint32_t[]> h_num_conn(new uint32_t[n_elems]);\n    std::unique_ptr<uint32_t[]> h_conn_idx(new uint32_t[n_elems * MAX_CONNECTIONS]);\n    std::unique_ptr<val_t[]> h_conn_flux(new // Ensure device arrays are non-empty even for a mesh with no connections\n    max_conn = std::max<idx_t>(max_conn, 1);\n\n    lap(\"repack\");\n    // Allocate and upload device buffers\n    uint32_t* d_num_conn = nullptr;\n    uint32_t* d_conn_idx = nullptr;\n    val_t* d_conn_flux = nullptr;\n    val_t* d_transfer = nullptr;\n    val_t* d_extflow = nullptr;\n    val_t* d_energy[2] = CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_extflow, n_elems * sizeof(val_t)));\n    for (int b = 0; b < 2; ++b) {\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_energy[b], n_elems * sizeof(val_t)));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_flux[b], n_elems * sizeof(val_t)));\n    }\n\n    lap(\"cudamalloc\");\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d_num_conn, h_num_conn.get(),\n                          n_elems * CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d_conn_flux, h_conn_flux.get(),\n                          n_elems * max_conn * sizeof(val_t), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d_transfer, CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d_flux[0], h_flux.get(),\n                          n_elems * sizeof(val_t), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n\n    lap(\"h2d\");\n    const int block_size = 256;\n    const int read = 1 - read;\n    }\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaDeviceSynchronize());\n    lap(\"kernels\");\n\n    // Download final state\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(h_energy.get(),          world.elements_dynamic[i].total_flux = h_flux[i];\n        }\n    });\n\n    lap(\"d2h\");\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_num_conn));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_conn_idx));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_conn_flux));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_transfer));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_extflow));\n    for (int b = 0; b < 2; ++b) {\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_energy[b]));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_flux[b]));\n    }\n}\n\n// Validate simulation results\nbool validateResults(const World& world) {\n    val_t energy_sum = 0.0;\n    val_t flux_sum = 0.0;\n    val_t energy_max = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::lowest();\n    val_t energy_min = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::max();\n    \n    for (const auto& elem : world.elements_dynamic) {\n        energy_sum += elem.current_energy;\n        flux_sum += elem.total_flux;\n        energy_max = std::max(elem.current_energy, energy_max);\n        energy_min = std::min(elem.current_energy, energy_min);\n    }\n    \n    printf(\"Validation results:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  Energy sum: %.12f\\n\", energy_sum);\n    printf(\"  Flux sum: %.2f\\n\", flux_sum);\n    printf(\"  Energy range: [%.6f, %.6f]\\n\", energy_min, energy_max);\n    \n    // Check for numerical issues\n    constexpr val_t energy_epsilon = 1e-8;\n    \n    if (!std::isfinite(energy_sum)) {\n        printf(\"  ERROR: Energy sum is not finite\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n    \n    if (std::abs(energy_sum) > energy_epsilon) {\n        printf(\"  WARNING: Energy sum diverged from 0 (expected conservation)\\n\");\n        // Don't fail validation as this can happen with external flows\n    }\n    \n    if (!std::isfinite(flux_sum)) {\n        printf(\"  ERROR: Flux sum is not finite\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n    \n    if (!std::isfinite(energy_max) || !std::isfinite(energy_min)) {\n        printf(\"  ERROR: Energy extrema are not finite\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n    \n    printf(\"  Validation: PASSED\\n\");\n    return true;\n}\n\n// Compute a simple hash of the results for verification\nuint64_t computeHash(const std::vector<ElementDynamic>& elements) {\n    uint64_t hash = 0;\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < elements.size(); ++i) {\n        // Simple hash combining energy and flux values\n        const uint64_t* e_ptr = reinterpret_cast<const uint64_t*>(&elements[i].current_energy);\n        const uint64_t* f_ptr = reinterpret_cast<const uint64_t*>(&elements[i].total_flux);\n        hash ^= (*e_ptr + i) * 0x9e3779b97f4a7c15ULL;\n        hash ^= (*f_ptr + i) * 0xbf58476d1ce4e5b9ULL;\n runSimulation(World& world, const int n_iters) {\n    auto tprev = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    auto lap = [&](const char* name){ auto now=std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now(); Only the slots\n    // actually used by the mesh (max connection count) are stored. The repack\n    // is embarrassingly parallel, so spread it over host threads.\n    // Thread spawn overhead dominates below ~1M elements, and ~16 threads\n    // saturate host memory bandwidth for // Ensure device arrays are non-empty even for a mesh with no connections\n    max_conn = std::max<idx_t>(max_conn, 1);\n\n    lap(\"repack\");\n    // Allocate and upload device buffers\n CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_flux[b], n_elems * sizeof(val_t)));\n    }\n\n    lap(\"cudamalloc\");\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d_num_conn, h_num_conn.get(),\n                          n_elems * CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_flux[b]));\n    }\n}\n\n// Validate simulation results\nbool validateResults(const World& world) {\n    val_t energy_sum = 0.0;\n    val_t flux_sum = 0.0;\n    val_t energy_max = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::lowest();\n    val_t energy_min = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::max();\n    \n    for (const auto& elem : world.elements_dynamic) {\n        energy_sum += elem.current_energy;\n        flux_sum += elem.total_flux;\n        energy_max = std::max(elem.current_energy, energy_max);\n        energy_min = std::min(elem.current_energy, energy_min);\n    }\n    \n    printf(\"Validation results:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  Energy sum: %.12f\\n\", energy_sum);\n    printf(\"  Flux sum: %.2f\\n\", flux_sum);\n    printf(\"  Energy range: [%.6f, %.6f]\\n\", finite\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n    \n    if (std::abs(energy_sum) > energy_epsilon) {\n        printf(\"  WARNING: Energy sum diverged from 0 (expected conservation)\\n\");\n        // Don't fail validation as this can happen with external flows\n    }\n    \n    if (!std::isfinite(flux_sum)) {\n        printf(\"  ERROR: Flux sum is not finite\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n    \n    if (!std::isfinite(energy_max) || !std::isfinite(energy_min)) {\n        printf(\"  ERROR: Energy extrema are not finite\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n    \n    printf(\"  Validation: PASSED\\n\");\n    return true;\n}\n\n// Compute a simple hash of the results for verification\nuint64_t computeHash(const std::vector<ElementDynamic>& elements) {\n    uint64_t hash = 0;\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < elements.size(); ++i) {\n        // Simple hash combining energy and flux values\n        const uint64_t* e_ptr = reinterpret_cast<const uint64_t*>(&elements[i].current_energy);\n        const uint64_t* f_ptr = reinterpret_cast<const uint64_t*>(&elements[i].total_flux);\n        hash ^= (*e_ptr + i) * 0x9e3779b97f4a7c15ULL;\n        hash ^= (*f_ptr + i) * 0xbf58476d1ce4e5b9ULL;\n    }\n    return hash;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Grid size (NxN Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    int n_elems_root = 512;\n    int n_iters = 10;\n a 2D square grid as an unstructured mesh\n// This represents computation on arbitrarily-shaped geometries\nvoid buildSquare2D(World& world, const int n_elems_root) {\n    const int n_elems = n_elems_root * n_elems_root;\n    \n    // Initialize materials\n    world.materials.emplace_back(Material{0.8, 0.0});    // Default material\n    world.materials.emplace_back(Material{0.8, 0.5});    // Inflow material\n    world.materials.emplace_back(Material{0.8, -0.5});   // Outflow material\n    \n    // Allocate elements\n    world.elements_static.resize(n_elems);\n    world.elements_dynamic.resize(n_elems);\n    world.elements_dynamic_swap.resize(n_elems);\n    \n    // Initialize all elements with default material and zero energy\n    for (int i = 0; i < n_elems; world.elements_dynamic[i].total_flux = 0.0;\n    }\n    \n    // Build connectivity: each element connects to its neighbors in 2D grid\n    for (int x = 0; x < n_elems_root; ++x) {\n        for (int y connection_flux, val_t other_energy) {\n    return (other_energy - this_energy) * transfer_coeff * connection_flux * 0.25;\n}\n\n// Update one element per thread for a single iteration.\n// Connectivity is stored in structure-of-arrays layout (stride n_elems per\n// connection slot) so warp accesses are coalesced.\n__global__ void updateElementsKernel(const uint32_t* __restrict__ num_connections,\n                                     const uint32_t* __restrict__ conn_idx,\n                                     const val_t* __restrict__ conn_flux,\n fprintf(stderr, \"  [%s] %.2f ms\\n\", name, std::chrono::duration<double,std::milli>(now-tprev).count()); tprev=now; };\n    const idx_t n_elems = world.elements_static.size();\n\n    // Repack Only the slots\n    // actually used by the mesh (max connection count) are stored. The repack\n    // is embarrassingly parallel, so spread it over host threads.\n    // Thread spawn overhead dominates below ~1M elements, and ~16 threads\n    // saturate host memory bandwidth for this streaming repack.\n    const size_t n_threads = std::max<size_t>(\n        1, std::min<size_t>({16, std::thread::hardware_concurrency(),\n                             n_elems / (256 * 1024)}));\n th.join();\n    };\n\n    // Allocate staging arrays uninitialized: the parallel repack below does\n    // the first touch, which keeps page fault-in parallel and NUMA-local.\n    // The connection std::unique_ptr<uint32_t[]> h_num_conn(new uint32_t[n_elems]);\n    std::unique_ptr<uint32_t[]> h_conn_idx(new uint32_t[n_elems * MAX_CONNECTIONS]);\n    std::unique_ptr<val_t[]> h_conn_flux(new world.materials[es.material_idx];\n            h_num_conn[i] = static_cast<uint32_t>(es.num_connections);\n            local_max = std::max(local_max, es.num_connections);\n            for (idx_t j =        }\n            h_transfer[i] = mat.transfer_coeff;\n            h_extflow[i] = mat.external_flow;\n            h_energy[i] = world.elements_dynamic[i].current_energy;\n            h_flux[i] = world.elements_dynamic[i].total_flux;\n        }\n        thread_max_conn[t] = local_max;\n    });\n    idx_t max_conn = 0;\n    for (idx_t m : thread_max_conn) max_conn = std::max(max_conn, m);\n    // Ensure device arrays are non-empty even for a mesh with no connections\n    max_conn = std::max<idx_t>(max_conn, 1);\n\n    lap(\"repack\");\n    // Allocate and upload device buffers\n    uint32_t* d_num_conn = nullptr;\n    uint32_t* d_conn_idx = nullptr;\n    val_t* d_conn_flux = nullptr;\n    val_t* d_transfer = nullptr;\n    val_t* d_extflow = nullptr;\n    val_t* d_energy[2] = {nullptr, nullptr};\n    val_t* d_flux[2] = {nullptr, nullptr};\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_num_conn, n_elems * sizeof(uint32_t)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_conn_idx, n_elems * max_conn * sizeof(uint32_t)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_conn_flux, n_elems * max_conn * sizeof(val_t)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_transfer, n_elems * sizeof(val_t)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_extflow, n_elems * sizeof(val_t)));\n    for (int b = 0; b < 2; ++b) {\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_energy[b], n_elems * sizeof(val_t)));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_flux[b], n_elems * sizeof(val_t)));\n    }\n\n    lap(\"cudamalloc\");\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d_num_conn, h_num_conn.get(),\n                          n_elems * CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d_conn_flux, h_conn_flux.get(),\n                          n_elems * max_conn * sizeof(val_t), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d_transfer, h_transfer.get(),\n                          n_elems * sizeof(val_t), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d_extflow, h_extflow.get(),\n                          n_elems * sizeof(val_t), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d_energy[0], h_energy.get(),\n                          n_elems * sizeof(val_t), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d_flux[0], h_flux.get(),\n                          n_elems * sizeof(val_t), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n\n    lap(\"h2d\");\n    const int block_size = 256;\n    const int block_size>>>(\n            d_num_conn, d_conn_idx, d_conn_flux, d_transfer, d_extflow,\n            d_energy[read], d_flux[read], d_energy[1 - read], d_flux[1 - read],\n            n_elems);\n        read = 1 - read;\n    }\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaDeviceSynchronize());\n    lap(\"kernels\");\n\n    // Download final state\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(h_energy.get(), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));\n    parallelFor([&](size_t, idx_t begin, idx_t end) {\n        for (idx_t i = begin; i < end; ++i) {\n            world.elements_dynamic[i].current_energy = h_energy[i];\n            world.elements_dynamic[i].total_flux = h_flux[i];\n        }\n    });\n\n    lap(\"d2h\");\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_num_conn));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_conn_idx));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_conn_flux));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_transfer));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_extflow));\n    for (int b = 0; b < 2; ++b) {\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_energy[b]));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_flux[b]));\n    }\n}\n\n// Validate simulation results\nbool validateResults(const World& world) {\n    val_t energy_sum = 0.0;\n    val_t flux_sum = 0.0;\n    val_t energy_max = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::lowest();\n    val_t energy_min = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::max();\n    \n    for (const auto& elem : world.elements_dynamic) {\n        energy_sum += elem.current_energy;\n        flux_sum += elem.total_flux;\n        energy_max = std::max(elem.current_energy, energy_max);\n        energy_min = std::min(elem.current_energy, energy_min);\n    }\n    \n    printf(\"Validation results:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  Energy sum: %.12f\\n\", energy_sum);\n    printf(\"  Flux sum: %.2f\\n\", flux_sum);\n    printf(\"  Energy range: [%.6f, %.6f]\\n\", energy_min, energy_max);\n    \n    // Check for numerical issues\n    constexpr val_t energy_epsilon = 1e-8;\n    \n    if (!std::isfinite(energy_sum)) {\n        printf(\"  ERROR: Energy sum is not finite\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n    \n    if (std::abs(energy_sum) > energy_epsilon) {\n        printf(\"  WARNING: Energy sum diverged from 0 (expected conservation)\\n\");\n        // Don't fail validation as this can happen with external flows\n    }\n    \n    if (!std::isfinite(flux_sum)) {\n        printf(\"  ERROR: Flux sum is not finite\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n    \n    if (!std::isfinite(energy_max) || !std::isfinite(energy_min)) {\n        printf(\"  ERROR: Energy extrema are not finite\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n    \n    printf(\"  Validation: PASSED\\n\");\n    return true;\n}\n\n// Compute a simple hash of the results for verification\nuint64_t computeHash(const std::vector<ElementDynamic>& elements) {\n    uint64_t hash = 0;\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < elements.size(); ++i) {\n        // Simple hash combining energy and flux values\n        const uint64_t* e_ptr = reinterpret_cast<const uint64_t*>(&elements[i].current_energy);\n        const uint64_t* f_ptr = reinterpret_cast<const uint64_t*>(&elements[i].total_flux);\n        hash ^= (*e_ptr + i) * 0x9e3779b97f4a7c15ULL;\n        hash ^= (*f_ptr + i) * 0xbf58476d1ce4e5b9ULL;\n    }\n    return hash;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Grid size (NxN Connectivity is stored in structure-of-arrays layout (stride n_elems per\n// connection slot) so warp accesses are coalesced.\n__global__ void updateElementsKernel(const uint32_t* __restrict__ The repack\n    // is embarrassingly parallel, so spread it over host threads.\n    // Thread spawn overhead dominates below ~1M elements, and ~16 threads\n    // saturate host memory bandwidth for this streaming repack.\n    const size_t n_threads = std::max<size_t>(\n        1, std::min<size_t>({16, std::thread::hardware_concurrency(),\n                             n_elems / (256 * 1024)}));\n        }\n            h_transfer[i] = mat.transfer_coeff;\n            h_extflow[i] = mat.external_flow;\n            h_energy[i] = world.elements_dynamic[i].current_energy;\n            h_flux[i] = world.elements_dynamic[i].total_flux;\n        }\n        thread_max_conn[t] = local_max;\n    });\n    idx_t max_conn = 0;\n    for (idx_t m : thread_max_conn) max_conn = std::max(max_conn, m);\n    // Ensure device arrays are non-empty even for a mesh with no connections\n    max_conn = std::max<idx_t>(max_conn, 1);\n\n    lap(\"repack\");\n    // Allocate and upload device buffers\n    uint32_t* d_num_conn = nullptr;\n    uint32_t* d_conn_idx = nullptr;\n    val_t* d_conn_flux = nullptr;\n    val_t* d_transfer = nullptr;\n    val_t* d_extflow = nullptr;\n    val_t* d_energy[2] = {nullptr, nullptr};\n    val_t* d_flux[2] = {nullptr, nullptr};\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_num_conn, n_elems * sizeof(uint32_t)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_conn_idx, n_elems * max_conn * CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_extflow, n_elems * sizeof(val_t)));\n    for (int b = 0; b < 2; ++b) {\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_energy[b], n_elems * sizeof(val_t)));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_flux[b], n_elems * sizeof(val_t)));\n    }\n\n    lap(\"cudamalloc\");\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d_num_conn, h_num_conn.get(),\n                          n_elems *          world.elements_dynamic[i].total_flux = h_flux[i];\n        }\n    });\n\n    lap(\"d2h\");\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_num_conn));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_conn_idx));\n CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_flux[b]));\n    }\n}\n\n// Validate simulation results\nbool validateResults(const World& world) {\n    val_t energy_sum = 0.0;\n    val_t flux_sum = 0.0;\n    val_t energy_max = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::lowest();\n    val_t energy_min = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::max();\n    \n    for (const auto& elem : world.elements_dynamic) {\n        energy_sum += elem.current_energy;\n        flux_sum += elem.total_flux;\n        energy_max = std::max(elem.current_energy, energy_max);\n        energy_min = std::min(elem.current_energy, energy_min);\n    }\n    \n    printf(\"Validation results:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  Energy sum: %.12f\\n\", energy_sum);\n    printf(\"  Flux sum: %.2f\\n\", flux_sum);\n    printf(\"  Energy range: [%.6f, %.6f]\\n\", finite\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n    \n    if (std::abs(energy_sum) > energy_epsilon) {\n        printf(\"  WARNING: Energy sum diverged from 0 (expected conservation)\\n\");\n        // Don't fail validation as this can happen with external flows\n    }\n    \n    if (!std::isfinite(flux_sum)) {\n        printf(\"  ERROR: Flux sum is not finite\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n    \n    if (!std::isfinite(energy_max) || !std::isfinite(energy_min)) {\n        printf(\"  ERROR: Energy extrema are not finite\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n    \n    printf(\"  Validation: PASSED\\n\");\n    return true;\n}\n\n// Compute a simple hash of the results for verification\nuint64_t computeHash(const std::vector<ElementDynamic>& elements) {\n    uint64_t hash = 0;\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < elements.size(); ++i) {\n        // Simple hash combining energy and flux values\n        const uint64_t* e_ptr = reinterpret_cast<const uint64_t*>(&elements[i].current_energy);\n        const uint64_t* f_ptr = reinterpret_cast<const uint64_t*>(&elements[i].total_flux);\n        hash ^= (*e_ptr + i) * 0x9e3779b97f4a7c15ULL;\n        hash ^= (*f_ptr + i) * 0xbf58476d1ce4e5b9ULL;\n The repack runs on 16 host threads with parallel first-touch of uninitialized staging buffers, which matters on this 256-thread NUMA machine.\n- Runs one CUDA thread per element per iteration, double-buffering energy/flux arrays on the device and swapping pointers between kernel launches — no host round-trips during iteration.\n- Copies results back into `world.elements_dynamic` afterward, so validation, hashing, and `-r` output are untouched.\n- `main` gained only a pre-timer CUDA context warm-up so one-time driver setup isn't counted as computation time.\n\n**`unstructured/CMakeLists.txt`** — compiles the existing `.cpp` as CUDA (`set_source_files_properties ... LANGUAGE CUDA`, `CMAKE_CUDA_ARCHITECTURES native`), with `-O3` and `-fmad=false` so per-operation arithmetic matches the IEEE evaluation order of the source expression. Executable name is still `unstructured`; no new files or dependencies.\n\n## Correctness\n\nResult hashes are bitwise identical to the original serial program (rebuilt separately as a reference) across five configurations including odd sizes: `-n 512 -i 10`, `-n 100 -i 7`, `-n 513 -i 33`, `-n 2048 -i 100`, `-n 31 -i 1`. Validation passes and energy conservation holds. The per-element flux summation order is preserved exactly, so this holds by construction, not just by tolerance.\n\n## Performance (RTX 3090)\n\n- Kernel: **0.44 ms/iteration** at 2048² (4.2M elements) — ~980 GB/s effective bandwidth, essentially the card's memory-bandwidth peak — vs ~41 ms/iteration serial (**~95× per iteration**).\n- End-to-end `-n 2048 -i 100`: 4.77 s → **0.27 s** (~18×; the remainder is one-time host repack and PCIe transfer, which I cut from ~990 ms to ~170 ms by eliminating serial page zeroing, trimming connection slots to the actual max, and parallelizing pack/unpack).\n\nOne pre-existing quirk left as-is for semantic equivalence: the printed GigaElements/GFLOPS metric overflows `int` for very large `n_elems × iterations` products (it did in the original code too); the timing and hash outputs are unaffected.","ttft_ms":6259,"type":"result","duration_ms":803312,"uuid":"f67de4b6-c235-4af2-95f9-593db3bba1f3","ttft_stream_ms":3256,"time_to_request_ms":75,"first_content_frame_ms":3256,"queued_turn_count":0,"result_index":0}
